Any references inside those verbatim rows to the former R-022 cap or R-026 minimum are historical R2 audit context, not current first-wave assignment; current identities resolve only through the R3 register. The 57 affected judgments are copied byte-for-byte from the approved replacement audit; their "new" label is now applied R3. Each replacement has29 counterpart checks, with their shared pair counted once. Source keys [Hxx]/[M]/[E/P]/[C] resolve through the respective unchanged provenance source registers; unchanged28 definitions have the same experimental semantics in current R3.

**BTC C-012/R-005 qualification:** their BTC selections are nested/highly dependent because aligned unambiguous1h structure can imply compatible context sign. The overall MEDIUM label reflects the nine non-BTC subjects' different own-structure/external-context questions; it is not independent BTC confirmation. C-015's BTC sign predicate may be nonbinding with a valid zero reference mean. No combined C-012+C-015 configuration is studied and no baseline reuse is independent replication.
